<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: center; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 3pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">PART I</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 3pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">&#160;</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold"><a name="Item1.Business"><!--Anchor--></a>Item&#160;1.&#160;&#160;Business</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">&#160;<font style="FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">Overview</font></div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We are engaged in the development, integration and marketing of rugged mobile personal computer systems, or PCs. Our rugged tablet PCs are designed to withstand hazardous conditions, such as extreme temperatures, driving rain, repeated vibrations, dirt, dust and concussive shocks.&#160; The intrinsically safe, ruggedized and reliable nature of our products facilitates the extension of traditional computing systems to a broader range of field personnel, including energy pipeline inspectors, public safety responders, warehouse workers and pharmaceutical scientists. Our tablets are fitted with a range of performance-matched accessories, including multiple docking solutions, wireless connectivity alternatives, global positioning system modules, biometric and smartcard options, as well as traditional peripherals, such as keyboards and cases.&#160; Additionally, our most rugged tablets are waterproof for up to 30 minutes in water up to a depth of three feet, are impervious to drops from as high as seven feet, are readable in direct sunlight, can be mounted on vehicles and include LTE and Wi-Fi connectivity options for real-time data access.&#160; Our end user customers include major telecommunications companies, leading heavy equipment manufacturers, oil and gas production companies, the military and first responders.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Until 2014, we competed and derived our revenue through the sale of our iX104 tablets in a subset of the rugged PC market, given the larger size and ultra-rugged attributes of our iX104 tablets, which weighs approximately 5.4 pounds.&#160; Since then, we have reduced our dependency upon the continued market acceptance of our iX104 systems by broadening the market for our products and increasing our opportunities for revenue growth.&#160; We have broadened our product offering, through development and acquisition, to include multiple fully-rugged and rugged tablets that are lighter weight and less expensive than our iX104 line of products.&#160; These new products have allowed us to compete in significantly larger segments of the rugged PC market.&#160; In 2014, we introduced our first fully-rugged, lighter weight tablets, offering Android and Windows operating systems, and in 2015 we completed the acquisition of certain assets of Motion Computing, Inc. and its subsidiaries, which we refer to herein as Motion, more fully discussed below.&#160; This acquisition expanded our product lines to include multiple additional rugged tablet products, and expanded our domestic and international customer base.&#160; We believe these lighter and less expensive tablets are ideal for field service applications and significantly more mobile market opportunities, as compared to our iX104.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Looking forward, our strategy is to build increased marketplace awareness of our product offerings, in an effort that we believe will enable us to increase our revenue and to expand our share of the markets addressed by those products.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We believe we are positioned for future revenue growth in the markets in which we compete.&#160; We have introduced a line of computers that, based upon third&#8209;party certifications, surpasses the performance standards and specifications that have been the accepted measuring sticks for rugged tablet computers in today&#8217;s marketplace.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our key initiatives for future revenue growth include the following:</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We are a Delaware corporation and our common stock trades on The NASDAQ Stock Market under the symbol &#8220;XPLR.&#8221;</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">Recent Developments</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">On April 17, 2015, we completed the acquisition of certain of the assets of Motion.&#160; Pursuant to the terms of a Foreclosure Purchase and Sale Agreement with Motion and Square 1 Bank, Motion&#8217;s senior secured lender, our subsidiary, Xplore Technologies Corporation of America, purchased certain of Motion&#8217;s assets, including cash, cash equivalents, accounts receivable, inventory, equipment, personal property and other assets, which we refer to herein as the Motion Acquisition.&#160;</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Also on April 17, 2015, in connection with the Motion Acquisition, our subsidiary entered into a Loan and Security Agreement with Square 1 Bank, pursuant to which Square 1 Bank provided us with formula and non-formula revolving loans for up to an aggregate amount of $15 million, which replaced our existing Accounts Receivable Purchase Agreement with DSCH Capital Partners, LLC d/b/a Far West Capital.&#160; On April 17, 2015, we borrowed approximately $9 million under this facility to fund the cash portion of the purchase price for the Motion Acquisition.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">On April 17, 2017, the aforementioned Loan and Security Agreement with Square 1 Bank matured and was replaced by a Loan and Security Agreement with Bank of America N.A., pursuant to which Bank of America provided us with a revolving asset based line of credit up to $15 million.&#160; The new facility with Bank of America has a three-year term, is secured by substantially all of our assets, and bears interest at LIBOR plus between 2.25% and 2.75% per annum, based upon our fixed charge coverage ratio.&#160; The balance of drawn funds at any one time cannot exceed the lesser of $15 million or the sum (1) 85% of our eligible accounts receivable and (2) the lesser of (a) 65% of the carried value of eligible inventory or (b) 85% of the liquidation value of eligible inventory, minus certain reserves.&#160; The maximum amount of eligible receivables comprised of accounts receivable outside of the United States of America and Canada is 25% of the balance of eligible accounts receivable.&#160; As of June 9, 2017, we had drawn $631,000 on this line of credit.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">In fiscal year 2016, we introduced two new products, the XSLATE B10 and the XSLATE D10, which was a replacement for our former RangerX product.&#160; In fiscal year 2017, we introduced the XSLATE R12, which was a replacement for the Motion R12 product.&#160; Our products are described in detail below.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">Products</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; FONT-STYLE: italic; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">iX104 Product Line</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We have spent more than a decade on research, development and product improvements with each generation of our iX104, achieving what we believe are a number of industry &#8220;firsts,&#8221; such as being the first manufacturer to incorporate dual-mode inputs with active pen and finger touch capabilities.&#160; Our signature iX104 computers have consistently been recognized for their ruggedness, versatility and best-in-class technologies.&#160; We believe that we pioneered, and continue to offer, the best outdoor&#8209;readable display and wLAN wireless solutions.&#160; Our specially designed Dual Mode Sunlight Readable screen is viewable in challenging lighting conditions, including direct sunlight and dimly-lit environments, from virtually any angle.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We believe that our recent versions of the iX104 continued to introduce &#8220;industry firsts&#8221; and differentiating features.&#160; The iX104C product line includes a tool-less removable dual solid state drive (SSD) module, tool-less access to the SIM and MicroSD ports, and an ingress protection rating of IP 67 for submersion in water.&#160; The iX104C product line also features the Intel Core i7 processor and runs on Windows 7, 8 and 10 operating systems.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our line of iX104 tablet PCs is designed to operate in challenging work environments, including extreme temperatures, constant vibrations, rain, blowing dirt and dusty conditions.&#160; Our iX104 systems can be fitted with a wide range of performance matched accessories, including multiple docking station solutions, wireless connectivity alternatives, Global Positioning System (GPS) modules, biometric and smartcard modules, as well as traditional peripherals like keyboards, mice and cases.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">In September of 2015 we launched the XSLATE B10 rugged tablet, which is targeted squarely at potential customers considering Panasonic&#8217;s most popular rugged tablet, the FX-G1.&#160;&#160;<font style="BACKGROUND-COLOR: #ffffff; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Built with unfailing protection in mind, the IP65 and MIL-STD-810G rated XSLATE B10 rugged tablet endures even the most extreme work environments. It also doesn&#8217;t compromise critical PC capabilities despite being only 2.4 lbs. We believe that t</font>he XSLATE<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller">&#160;</sup>B10 offers superior standard I/O features, a unique set of peripherals, and greater environmental resiliency than the Panasonic FX-G1.</div>

<div style="BACKGROUND-COLOR: #ffffff">
<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">The XSLATE B10 also addresses customers&#8217; requirements for more expansive and flexible I/O options, giving customers a standard that we believe is unmatched by competitive products.&#160; No other rugged tablet PC that we are aware of can deliver at least eight ports that are available all the time.&#160; The XSLATE B10 users can customize their I/O configurations to fit their unique workflow needs, with the following options:</div>

<div>&#160;</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">The Motion F5m and Motion C5m are easily recognized by their iconic integrated handle design, which was introduced by Motion in 2008.&#160; The Motion C5 was originally introduced in a white color for clinical applications.&#160; Motion then followed up with a black version of the Motion C5, the Motion F5, for field applications.&#160; <font style="BACKGROUND-COLOR: #ffffff; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">The rugged tablets&#8217; fifth generation Intel Core processors, including the highest performing Intel i7 vPro processor, provide the power to quickly move through computation-intensive applications, while increasing overall power efficiency for longer battery life.</font></div>

<div style="BACKGROUND-COLOR: #ffffff">
<div><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="BACKGROUND-COLOR: #ffffff; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">The products have a 10.4&#8221; View Anywhere&#160;display that mimics the size of many standard forms for which input is made easy with both touch and digital pen interfaces.&#160; An optional barcode scanner is integrated into the handle for easy push-button reads.&#160; A </font>high resolution digital camera and the &#8220;SnapWorks by Xplore&#8221; camera application make it easy to capture images, annotate those images with the pen, and share the data with other applications.&#160; An optional UHF RFID scanner enables long range reads of RFID tags up to nine feet away.&#160; And, the &#8220;EasyConnect&#8221; expansion module can be populated with a magstripe reader to accept payments in real-time at the point of service.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">The products are often used in large metropolitan areas where LTE networks are susceptible to traffic congestion that negatively impacts connectivity performance.&#160; To overcome that challenge, the products were designed and certified to support the additional spectrum of AWS (Advanced Wireless Services), commonly marketed by some carriers as XLTE.&#160;<br>
</div>

<div><br>
</div>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; FONT-STYLE: italic; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">Bobcat</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">At 2.4 pounds, the Bobcat is a fully-rugged Windows tablet with comprehensive standardized interfaces, an array of options through its proprietary Xpansion Port and a lightweight design. The Bobcat operates Windows 8.1 or Windows 7 Pro operating systems and is certified fully-rugged, with a MIL-STD 810G certification and IP65 rating.&#160; The Bobcat&#8217;s direct bonded IPS screen, 500 nits of luminance and an 800-to-1 contrast ratio provides for outdoor readability, and a 10-finger multi-touch screen provides users with ease-of-use and touch optimization. The Bobcat is used in a variety of industries, including manufacturing, warehousing and distribution, utilities, telecommunications and the military.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Bobcat offers a suite of standard inputs and outputs, enabling the transition across different business applications. The Bobcat comes with two full-sized USB 3.0s, Micro SDXC, Micro SIM, Micro HDMI-Out, RJ-45 Ethernet, GPS engine and a serial port connection. We believe that the Bobcat is the only fully-rugged tablet on the market that offers GPS, RJ-45 Ethernet and a serial port connection in one device.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We have developed a suite of optional features designed to optimize Bobcat for specific industries. Our proprietary Xpansion Port includes integration options such as a 1D/2D barcode scanner, common access card (CAC) reader and near-field communications (NFC) reader.&#160; Warehousing and distribution businesses can consolidate operations and reduce equipment costs with the Bobcat&#8217;s barcode scanner or NFC reader capabilities, while the military and other governmental entities use the CAC reader for increased security.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; FONT-STYLE: italic; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">XSLATE D10</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Launched in December of 2015, the XSLATE D10 is the follow on to our Android Ranger X.&#160; <font style="BACKGROUND-COLOR: #ffffff; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">The hardened exterior, doubly-powerful interior and &#8220;all ports, all the time&#8221; connectivity of the XSLATE D10 rugged tablet gives the Android&#160;5.1, Lollipop OS what we believe is a tough, new edge over its competition.</font></div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Unlike many other under-featured Android products, the XSLATE D10 is fully rugged with a broad range of purpose-built capabilities to support demanding workflows.&#160; The product is IP-65 rated for protection against rain, water and dust.&#160; Using the MIL-STD-810G protocol, it is rated for five foot drops.&#160; Elastomer bumpers, a chemically hardened display, and a stiff magnesium alloy midframe, help protect the XSLATE D10 against screen breakage.&#160; The product is also compliant with C1D2 and ATEX standards for hazardous locations and explosive environments.&#160; Similar to our XSLATE B10, the XSLATE D10 provides eight standard I/O ports, including 2x USB 3.0, Micro SDXC, Micro HDMI-out, and RJ45 Gigabit Ethernet connection and optional HDMI Input module.&#160; The XSLATE D10 is certified for 4G LTE networks and also is equipped with Wi-Fi and Bluetooth standard.&#160; Additionally, the product is compatible with our full range of G2-class accessories, including industrial and vehicle docks, companion keyboard, top handle, kickstand, barcode reader, CAC/SmartCard reader, NFC and HDMI-In modules.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">The XSLATE D10 also provides the power customers need, along with the battery life required for all day operation.&#160; It is powered by an Intel x86 Atom-series core processor with 4GB RAM and a 64GB SSD.&#160; It provides up to 20 hours of battery life with the optional hot-swappable second battery option, and leverages Google&#8217;s Project Volta and Android ultra-power saving mode for energy consumption savings.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our line of tablets offer the following features:</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Rugged</font>&#8212; We have designed and built our products from the inside out, developing design elements that provide a heightened and proven level of durability.&#160; Some of our products meet some of the strictest specifications in the world, such as those established by the U.S. military, including Military Standard Testing for Environmental Extremes.&#160; By being designed to meet these specifications, some of our products can withstand damage from being dropped onto concrete from a height of up to seven feet, from being submerged for up to 30 minutes in up to three feet of water, and from being exposed to extreme temperatures are as low as &#8722;60&#176; Fahrenheit and as high as 160&#176; Fahrenheit.&#160; In addition, some of our products are designed to continue to function when subjected to vibration, sand storms and other challenging outdoor work environments.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Screen Technology </font>&#8212; We strive to be a leader in developing screen technology with award winning displays.&#160; We have designed our &#8220;Sunlight Readable&#8221; screen to be viewable in challenging lighting conditions, including direct sunlight and dimly-lit environments, at virtually any angle, and to enable glove touch capability.&#160; Our screens also offer Dual Mode inputs&#8212;simultaneous use of a digital pen and/or finger to control the unit.&#160; The Dual Mode supports more precise inputs through the pen with more directional finger touch inputs&#8212;all in a single unit with automatic switching capabilities.&#160;</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Processing Power </font>&#8212; We have the ability to provide processing power alternatives for our products on a timely and cost-effective basis.&#160; Our systems use Intel processors and associated chipsets, as well as other performance enhancement technologies that we believe are essential in many field applications (such as mapping and remote connectivity).&#160;</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Remote Connectivity</font>&#8212; Our tablet PCs have a range of wireless communications options (wLAN, Bluetooth, 4G LTE and NFC) as well as two meter and sub-meter GPS options.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">&#160;<font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Accessories&#8212;</font> We offer a broad range of add-on modules and accessories that we believe better enable our customers to adapt our tablet PCs to their intended use.&#160; In particular, we believe our functional, durable and reliable docking solutions are tailored to our customers&#8217; needs.&#160; We have supplied service, desktop, vehicle, forklift, armored vehicle and mobile cart docking systems to our customers.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Heightened Safety Standards&#8212; </font>Some models of our line of wireless-enabled tablet PC systems have been tested and certified both in North America and in the European Union for use in hazardous conditions.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our tablet PCs are designed to be used as a mobile computing system.&#160; These systems are comprised of a hardware platform that is fully integrated with one or more software applications.&#160; Through its wide feature set, we believe our line of products allows for the customization of a platform that best suits a given application.&#160; Our computers combine processing power, viewability, ruggedness and connectivity, and are designed to operate in extreme environments.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">Strategy</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our strategy is to become the leading developer and marketer of rugged mobile wireless computer systems.&#160; We currently compete in the rugged tablet PC market.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; FONT-STYLE: italic; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Continue Expansion into New Rugged Product Markets</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We have successfully broadened our offerings and opportunity by development of new products and through the Motion Acquisition.&#160; During fiscal year 2017, we launched the award winning XSLATE R12, and also received the largest single order in our history for our flagship Android fully rugged tablet, the XSLATE D10.&#160;We believe, based upon the 2016 annual white paper published by the Mobile and Wireless Practice of Venture Development Corporation, or VDC, that an increasing number of operations are requiring their employees to transact business in the field and/or other non-traditional office environments.&#160;&#160;The research paper published by VDC projected worldwide sales in the rugged mobile computing market to grow to over $7.8&#160;billion by the end of calendar 2017, and for the market for large form factor rugged devices to total $2.2&#160;billion for calendar 2016.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We believe that our lines of rugged tablet PCs are uniquely positioned to capitalize on the convergence of four current market trends:</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We believe that many enterprises are coming to the realization that the total cost of computer ownership is improved through the use of rugged computing solutions.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">The Motion Acquisition has also opened up larger international markets to us through the addition of a multi-country sales team and new international distributor and reseller relationships.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; FONT-STYLE: italic; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Leverage Existing Markets</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We seek to continue to analyze the needs of the vertical markets that we are currently addressing, so that we can continue to increase our sales in those markets.&#160; We intend to continue to focus on customer specific applications by leveraging our core products and technology, as well as our key strategic alliances.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: justify; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our strategy includes the following key elements:&#160;</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Identifying and targeting vertical markets, major account and OEM opportunities</font>&#8212; To achieve broad market penetration by our products, we intend to continue to focus on specific vertical market applications, major accounts and OEM relationships.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Outsourcing manufacturing and nurturing of key relationships&#8212; </font>We intend to continue to outsource our manufacturing function, so that we can continue to focus our resources on our technology and product development, customer application and project deployment activities, through our collaborations on engineering and manufacturing matters with our contract manufacturers.&#160; In addition, we plan to continue nurturing a number of key distributor and reseller relationships.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: justify; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Flexible product design and customer-centric approach</font>&#8212; We believe that the design of our products provides us with the flexibility to respond to customer&#8209;specific requirements.&#160; We involve our customers in our product development and enhancement efforts.&#160; This approach is intended to result in improved communication throughout the entire sales cycle, and is designed to position our products as the optimal mobile computing platform for our customers.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: justify; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Delivery of high quality, reliable systems</font>&#8212; We measure and seek to improve product quality through rigorous quality assurance programs implemented through our strategic alliances, in concert with performing our custom&#8209;designed test programs.&#160; Additionally, we utilize feedback provided by our customers.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: justify; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN: 3pt 0.2pt 6pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Marketing and distribution relationships</font>&#8212; Within each targeted vertical market, we intend to focus on entering into co-marketing relationships with key application providers and systems integrators.&#160; This strategy is designed to allow us to use multiple sales channels within a region, while maintaining key strategic alliances.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">Sales</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our direct customers primarily consist of distribution companies, such as large computer companies, specialized system integrators, software vendors, distributors and value-added resellers, and to a lesser extent the end-users.&#160;&#160;For fiscal year 2017, approximately 93% of our total revenues were attributable to sales through our distribution and reseller channels, and approximately 7% of our total revenues were attributable to sales directly to end-users.&#160;&#160;We currently have relationships with more than 120 distributors and direct resellers.&#160;&#160;Our distributors and resellers generally have large sales organizations that, in turn, sell our products to entities that are the ultimate end-users.&#160; In any given year, a single distributor may account for a significant portion of our revenue.&#160;&#160;In fiscal year 2017, we had two resellers located in the United States, SHI International and Synnex Information Technologies Inc., who accounted for approximately 20% and 16% of our total revenue, respectively.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">As of March 31, 2017, we had a sales team of nineteen individuals that either have geographic responsibilities for direct and indirect sales opportunities or responsibilities for specific enterprise customers, without limitation by geography.&#160;&#160;Our regional and international sales teams work closely with our distributors and resellers in defined regions.&#160; Our enterprise sales team works on specific large accounts and industry verticals. &#160;Our distributors are currently selling our products into the public safety, utility, telecommunications, field service, warehousing logistics, transportation, oil and gas production, manufacturing, route delivery, military and homeland security markets.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our North American revenue was approximately 76% of our total revenue in fiscal year 2017, as compared to approximately 73% of total revenue in fiscal year 2016.&#160;&#160;The significance of the North American revenue was primarily attributable to large volume orders from our large medical, military and telecommunications end-users located in the United States.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We have significantly expanded our distribution capacity by adding the Motion distribution channel, which had been developed over a period of over ten years.&#160; Motion sold its products into the healthcare, public safety, construction, utility and retail markets.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">Marketing</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">&#160;There are three primary areas of focus for our marketing effort:</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">
<div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; FONT-STYLE: italic; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Brand Awareness and Lead Generation</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our brand awareness and lead generation initiatives use digital media, are focused on industry vertical markets, and leverage our channel distributors to amplify our message in the market.&#160; Key elements of these initiatives include:</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; FONT-STYLE: italic; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Channel Development</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our go-to-market strategy is channel driven, and includes distributors that enable quick product delivery and third party product configuration, direct and indirect IT resellers with rugged mobility expertise in key verticals, systems integrators that may bring elements such as wireless communication systems to a project, installation and deployment companies, as well as application software suppliers that service our target verticals.&#160; Our channel development efforts are focused on identifying and recruiting distributors that add value to our customer deployments, and provide the tools and resources that enable them to be successful in positioning our products and designing and deploying world-class solutions for our shared end-customers.&#160; To achieve this, we:</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; FONT-STYLE: italic; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Product Marketing</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our product marketing work starts with aligning our product development plans with the anticipated needs of our target customers.&#160; We recognize that, as a smaller company, our key to success depends on our ability to provide better products than our larger competitors, and to be more responsive to our customers&#8217; needs.&#160; As such, our product innovations are grounded in combining emerging technologies with customer needs.&#160; When embarking on the development of a new product or an upgrade of an existing one, we devote resources to engaging customers, distributors and industry experts in the design process.&#160; We believe that this process, combined with our flexibility to make quick decisions with the support of our contract manufacturers, has enabled us to deliver products with market leading technology ahead of our competitors.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">The market prices for rugged computers are higher than that for commercial grade computers used in traditional office settings.&#160; However, we believe that the total cost of ownership of a rugged computer over a three to five-year period can be significantly lower than the cost of a cheaper non-rugged computer in certain environments.&#160; Several of our customers have disclosed to us in our customer&#8209;based market research studies that they experienced higher direct costs using non-rugged devices, with&#160;more frequent damage, information retrieval costs and replacement costs, as well as higher indirect costs, such as prolonged downtime.&#160; This conclusion is also supported by third-party research.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; FONT-STYLE: italic; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Market Segments</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We target a number of different market segments in which we believe the deployment of rugged mobile computers can greatly improve operating efficiencies, reduce related costs, and improve customer service levels.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Telecommunications, Utilities &amp; Energy</font>.&#160; Generally, telecom, utility and energy related companies continuously have to respond to customers&#8217; requests for service and infrastructure maintenance expeditiously and efficiently to remain competitive.&#160; We believe that the reliable and real-time movement of information to and from the field is vital to the success of any field automation system.&#160; To fully leverage the value of mobile computing in these environments, they must also be safe to use in hazardous environments where explosive gases may be present &#8211; particularly relevant to the oil and gas industry.&#160; We have responded with products that are compliant with C1D2 and ATEX standards for use in these environments.&#160; Two global top ten telecommunications providers in the world are end-users of our products, as well as several of the world&#8217;s largest utility and energy companies, including Hydro One in Canada, EDF in France, RWE in Germany, Sydney Water in Australia, and Thames Water in the United Kingdom.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 40.5pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Military.</font>&#160; As the military continues to transition to commercial and industrial grade rugged mobile computing systems, we expect this market segment will represent a significant opportunity for our products.&#160; In particular, we believe the U.S. Department of Defense is generally moving away from full military specifications adherence, except for system&#8209;critical operations, and instead is increasing emphasis on purchasing commercial, off-the-shelf (COTS) equipment.&#160; The military market sector includes ground and C4I (Command, Control, Communications, Computers and Intelligence) systems.&#160; We have deployed solutions across the Army, Air Force, and Navy in the U. S., as well as military operations in other countries.&#160;</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 10pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 10pt; CLEAR: both" id="DSPFPageBreakArea">
<div id="DSPFPageFooter"></div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: center" id="DSPFPageNumberArea"><font style="FONT-STYLE: normal; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 8pt; FONT-WEIGHT: normal" id="DSPFPageNumber">9</font></div>

<div style="PAGE-BREAK-AFTER: always" id="DSPFPageBreak">
<hr style="BORDER-RIGHT-WIDTH: 0px; BACKGROUND-COLOR: #000000; MARGIN: 4px 0px; WIDTH: 100%; BORDER-TOP-WIDTH: 0px; BORDER-BOTTOM-WIDTH: 0px; HEIGHT: 2px; COLOR: #000000; CLEAR: both; BORDER-LEFT-WIDTH: 0px" noshade="noshade">

<div><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Public Safety.</font> Given the focus in the U.S. on homeland security matters and the continued commitment by Federal, state and municipal governments on law enforcement, fire and emergency medical services, members of the public safety sector are searching for efficiencies that will better enable them to do their jobs.&#160; Rugged mobile computing devices assist these groups in a variety of ways.&#160; For example, having a reliable and durable tablet PC provides law enforcement agencies with immediate and reliable access in the field to national and local criminal databases.&#160; In this market segment, our products have been sold to hundreds of public safety organizations across the world, including the Abu Dhabi Police, Danish Ambulance Service, and the German Red Cross.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Manufacturing.</font>&#160; <font style="BACKGROUND-COLOR: #ffffff; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Manufacturing floor environments demand resiliency and mobility, and require more rugged computing power than ever before, to store large amounts of data and display complex and highly detailed drawings.&#160; Whether assembly, maintenance, or repair operations are confined to an indoor factory, an enclosed hangar, or an outdoor testing facility, the environmental elements and vibrations from equipment and forklifts can be harmful to the average laptop, desktop, or mobile PC.&#160; Our rugged tablet IP ratings and the results of extensive MIL-STD-810G tests are particularly relevant to this segment.&#160; We have experienced particular success in the Automotive and Aerospace sectors of this market segment, with customers that include Boeing, Bombardier in Canada, Embraer in Brazil, Dassault Aviation in France, Audi in Germany, and Volkswagen in Italy.</font></div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"><font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Transportation and Distribution.</font>&#160;&#160;We believe that globalization, increased competition and heightened consumer expectations are contributing factors to the adoption of mobile computing technologies by many leading transportation and distribution companies.&#160; <font style="BACKGROUND-COLOR: #ffffff; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">That means mobilizing highly complex and integrated logistical tasks in a way that is simple and straightforward, whether on planes, trains, forklifts or tractor-trailers.&#160; </font>These operations typically require real time price modifications, product introductions and transitions, timely inventory management, load balancing and scheduling.&#160; We believe that this market segment will continue to automate order fulfillment, inventory control and management systems as part of an overall effort to integrate enterprise resource planning and supply chain management information systems.&#160; Our end-users include leading railroad, trucking and airline companies, package delivery companies, as well as the warehouse operations of large automotive manufacturing and food processing plants.&#160; We have a diverse set of customers in this segment, including Norfolk Southern, CSX, UPS, and Hertz.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">Research and Development</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We have assembled an experienced engineering and product development team.&#160; Through the collaboration of our employees and the engineering teams of our Taiwan-based contract manufacturers, we believe we are able to bring significant resources to the research, development and design of our products.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We seek to design and manage product life cycles through a controlled and structured process.&#160; We involve customers and industry experts from our target markets in the definition and refinement of our product development.&#160; Product development emphasis is placed on meeting industry standards and product specifications, ease of integration, ease of use, cost reduction, design-for manufacturability, quality and reliability.&#160;</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We continue to invest in research and development to enhance and expand our rugged mobile computing systems.&#160;&#160;We are considering additional form factors, operating systems and screen technologies for integration into our rugged platforms as we seek to expand into additional markets.&#160;&#160;During the fiscal years ended March&#160;31, 2017 and 2016, we expended $4,462,000 and $5,771,000, respectively, on research and development activities, none of which was borne by our customers.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 10pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 10pt; CLEAR: both" id="DSPFPageBreakArea">

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: center" id="DSPFPageNumberArea"><font style="FONT-STYLE: normal; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 8pt; FONT-WEIGHT: normal" id="DSPFPageNumber">10</font></div>

</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">Competition</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Competition in our industry is intense and is characterized by rapidly changing technologies, evolving industry standards, frequent new product introductions and rapid changes in customer requirements.&#160; To be competitive, we must continue to develop and introduce, on a timely and cost-effective basis, new products and product features that keep pace with technological developments and emerging industry standards, and address the increasingly sophisticated needs of our end users.&#160; We believe that the principal competitive factors affecting the market for our products are the product&#8217;s performance, features and reliability, price, customer service, reputation in the industry and brand loyalty.&#160; We believe that our strongest competitive advantages are our products&#8217; durability and reputation in the industry.&#160; In order to compete, we will be required to continue to respond promptly and effectively to the challenges of technological changes and our competitors&#8217; innovations.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our primary competitors in the mobile rugged computer market include the following:</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Panasonic and Getac have more product offerings and greater financial, technical, and research and development resources and marketing capabilities than we do.&#160; DRS produces tablets with specific connectivity required by the U.S. Military.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We also face competition from manufacturers of non-rugged mobile computers, such as Samsung, Inc., Dell,&#160;Inc., Hewlett&#8209;Packard Company, Apple,&#160;Inc., Sony and Toshiba, to the extent customers decide to purchase less expensive traditional computers for use in environments that we believe are better suited for mobile rugged computers.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">Manufacturing</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We have a scalable manufacturing infrastructure to support our growing business.&#160; Our primary contract manufacturers, Wistron Corporation (Wistron), Pegatron Corporation (Pegatron) and Ubiqconn Technology, Inc. (Ubiqconn), are located in Taiwan.&#160; Wistron is the supplier of our iX104 and XSLATE B10 products.&#160; Wistron is recognized as a leading provider of computers and electronic components to some of the world&#8217;s largest technology companies, including Dell,&#160;Inc. and Hewlett&#8209;Packard Company.&#160; Pegatron is the supplier of our XSLATE R12, F5/C5 and CL920 products.&#160; Pegatron, is also a leading provider of computers and electronic components to the world&#8217;s largest technology companies.&#160; Ubiqconn is the supplier of our XSLATE D10 and Bobcat products.&#160; Ubiqconn was spun off from the First International Computer (FIC) Group in 2011 and is a wholly-owned subsidiary of FIC.&#160; FIC has been a dominant force in the technology research, product development and manufacturing services since 1980.&#160; Ubiqconn is comprised of FIC&#8217;s Industrial Computer Business Unit and FIC&#8217;s original research and development team, which has accumulated over twenty years of experience in developing IT products for worldwide first tier companies.&#160; We expect our relationships with our contract manufacturers will support our expected sales growth and product demand for the foreseeable future.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We outsource the majority of our manufacturing services for our rugged mobile PC tablets to our contract manufacturers, including board production, parts procurement, assembly, some quality assurance testing, warranty repair and service.&#160; We have design and manufacturing agreements with our contract manufacturers and they collaborate with us on product specifications and provide us with the flexibility to make changes to our products as market conditions change.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div id="DSPFPageFooter"></div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: center" id="DSPFPageNumberArea"><font style="FONT-STYLE: normal; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 8pt; FONT-WEIGHT: normal" id="DSPFPageNumber">11</font></div>

<div><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Under the terms of our agreements with our contract manufacturers, they provide us with design, manufacturing and support services related to our rugged mobile PC tablets.&#160; The purchase price of our products is determined based on the specific configuration of the tablet PC being produced, and is subject to cost reduction plans and volume based discounts.&#160; At least quarterly, we meet with each of the contract manufacturers to develop cost reduction plans.&#160; The plans take into account alternative suppliers along with components, design, process changes and other cost savings procedures.&#160; Each month we provide the contract manufacturers with a six month rolling forecast of the products we anticipate ordering.&#160; Generally, each of the contract manufacturers have approximately 90&#160;days after its acceptance of our purchase order to ship the product.&#160; If products ordered during any quarter exceed the volume projected in the forecast, each of the contract manufacturers has agreed to use its reasonable best efforts to deliver the excess products within 45&#160;days after its acceptance of the applicable purchase order.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our contract manufacturers provide several warranties to us, including that they have all necessary rights required to sell the products, that each product will be free from any material defect, that the products will be free from any liens, encumbrances or defects in title and that the products will comply with all specifications.&#160; The current terms of our agreements with the contract manufacturers are for one year and automatically renew for additional one year terms, unless either party provides written notice of its intent to terminate the agreement at least 90&#160;days prior to the expiration of any renewal term.&#160; In addition, the agreements contain provisions that allow for termination for any reason by either party upon 120&#160;days&#8217; notice.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We purchase materials, supplies and product subassemblies for our rugged mobile personal computer tablets from a number of vendors.&#160; Some key components included in our line of products are currently available only from single or limited sources.&#160; In the past, we have experienced significant price increases and limited availability of certain components that are not available from multiple sources.&#160; We are dependent upon Microsoft Corporation for various software products, including products included in our rugged mobile PC tablets.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Like other participants in the computer manufacturing industry, we ordinarily acquire materials and components through a combination of blanket and scheduled purchase orders to support our requirements for periods averaging 90 to 150&#160;days.&#160; At times, we have been constrained by parts availability in meeting our product orders.&#160; From time to time, we have obtained scarce components for somewhat higher prices on the open market, which may have a negative impact on our gross margins on our products, but does not disrupt production.&#160; On occasion, we have also acquired component inventory from our suppliers in anticipation of supply constraints.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">Intellectual Property</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our performance and ability to compete are dependent to a significant degree on our proprietary technology.&#160; We rely primarily upon a combination of patent, copyright and trade secret laws and license agreements to establish and protect proprietary rights in our products and technology.&#160; On March 31, 2017, we had thirty-eight U.S. patents and thirteen non-US patents.&#160; In addition, we had one U.S. patent application, one Chinese patent application, one European Union patent application and one Taiwanese patent application, all related to proprietary elements of our tablet products, and one U.S. patent application, one Taiwanese patent application and one International PCT patent application related to our wireless dock.&#160; Of these patents a total of thirty-two US patents and thirteen non-U.S. patents were acquired in the Motion Acquisition.&#160; Even with patent protection, it may be possible for a third party to copy or otherwise obtain and use our products or technology without our authorization, or to develop similar technology independently.&#160; In addition, effective patent, copyright and trade secret protection may be unavailable or limited in certain foreign countries.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We do not believe that our products infringe on the proprietary rights of any third parties.&#160; There can be no assurance, however, that third parties will not claim such infringement by us or our licensees with respect to current or future products.&#160; In the past, we have had third parties assert exclusive patent, copyright, trademark or other intellectual property rights to technologies or marks that are important to our business.&#160; Any such claims, with or without merit, could be time consuming, result in costly litigation, cause product shipment delays or require us to enter into royalty or licensing agreements with the claimant, any of which could delay the development and commercialization of our products or increase the costs of our products.&#160; After the Motion Acquisition, three companies filed lawsuits against us and Motion for alleged infringement of the claimant&#8217;s patent by Motion&#8217;s products and our products.&#160; We have settled two of the lawsuits by entering into license agreements, with a modest one-time payment.&#160; We have no potential liability with the third claimant because we had, and continue to have, an existing license agreement with that company.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We work closely with our contract manufacturers to stay abreast of the latest developments in rugged mobile computer technology.&#160; We obtain patent licenses for some technologies, some of which require significant royalty payments, when we believe those licenses are necessary or advantageous to our business.&#160; We have entered into non-exclusive licensing arrangements with Microsoft and other software suppliers for various operating systems and application software that we sell with our rugged tablet PCs.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">Government Regulation</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our business is subject to regulation by various federal and state governmental agencies.&#160; Such regulation includes the radio frequency emission regulatory activities of the U.S. Federal Communications Commission, the anti-trust regulatory activities of the U.S. Federal Trade Commission and Department of Justice, the consumer protection laws of the Federal Trade Commission, the import/export regulatory activities of the U.S. Department of Commerce, the product safety regulatory activities of the U.S. Consumer Products Safety Commission and environmental regulation by a variety of regulatory authorities in each of the areas in which we conduct business.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">Employees</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">As of March&#160;31, 2017, we had 88 full-time employees, of which 45 were employed in the operations, engineering, research and development and customer support areas, 10 were involved in corporate, finance and administrative areas and 33 were employed in sales and marketing.&#160; We anticipate that we will need to hire additional employees going forward.&#160;&#160;Our employees are not represented by a union or other collective bargaining unit and we have never experienced a work stoppage.&#160;&#160;We believe that our employee relations are good.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">Trademarks and Service Marks</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Each trademark, trade name or service mark of any other company appearing in this Annual Report on Form 10-K belongs to its holder.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold"><a name="Item1A.Risk"><!--Anchor--></a>Item&#160;1A.&#160; Risk Factors</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">There are many risks that affect our business and results of operations, some of which are beyond our control.&#160; If any of the following risks actually occur, our business, financial condition or operating results could be materially harmed.&#160; This could cause the trading price of our common stock to decline, and you may lose all or part of your investment.&#160; Additional risks that we do not yet know of or that we currently think are immaterial may also affect our business and results of operations.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-WEIGHT: bold">Risks Relating to our Business</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; FONT-STYLE: italic; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We have a history of net losses and may never become profitable.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Until fairly recently, we have incurred net losses in each fiscal year since our inception.&#160;&#160;As of March&#160;31, 2017, our accumulated deficit was approximately $143 million, primarily due to our past losses.&#160;&#160;Historically, our losses have resulted primarily from expenses incurred in research and development of our technology and products and from selling and marketing our products.&#160;&#160;We may continue to incur additional operating losses as we continue our research and development efforts, introduce new products and expand our sales and marketing activities.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">In addition, prior to the Motion Acquisition, Motion incurred net losses in each fiscal year since its inception.&#160;&#160;For its fiscal year ended December 31, 2014, Motion had a net loss of approximately $9 million and, as of December 31, 2014, its accumulated deficit was approximately $128&#160;million, primarily due to its past losses.&#160;&#160;</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 10pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 10pt; CLEAR: both" id="DSPFPageBreakArea">
<div id="DSPFPageFooter"></div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: center" id="DSPFPageNumberArea"><font style="FONT-STYLE: normal; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 8pt; FONT-WEIGHT: normal" id="DSPFPageNumber">13</font></div>

<div style="PAGE-BREAK-AFTER: always" id="DSPFPageBreak">
<hr style="BORDER-RIGHT-WIDTH: 0px; BACKGROUND-COLOR: #000000; MARGIN: 4px 0px; WIDTH: 100%; BORDER-TOP-WIDTH: 0px; BORDER-BOTTOM-WIDTH: 0px; HEIGHT: 2px; COLOR: #000000; CLEAR: both; BORDER-LEFT-WIDTH: 0px" noshade="noshade">
</div>

<div style="WIDTH: 100%" id="DSPFPageHeader">
<div><a href="#TableofContents">Table of Contents<br>
</a></div>
</div>
</div>

<div><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Since the Motion Acquisition, our operating expenses have more than doubled.&#160; We also incurred additional expenses beginning in fiscal 2016 related to <font style="BACKGROUND-COLOR: #ffffff; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">interest payments on the indebtedness we incurred in connection with the Motion Acquisition, amortization of intangible assets associated with the acquisition, other related acquisition, integration and financing costs, and related depreciation and other amortization.&#160; W</font>e cannot assure you that our revenue will be adequate to offset these increased expenses, which may<font style="BACKGROUND-COLOR: #ffffff; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt"> keep us from being profitable in the future.&#160; We cannot assure you that we will generate net profits from operations in fiscal 2018 or subsequent years.</font>&#160;&#160;We cannot assure you that our revenue will increase or that we will be profitable in any future period.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; FONT-STYLE: italic; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">In fiscal year 2017, we derived more than 10% of our revenue from two different customers.&#160;&#160;If we are unable to replace revenues generated from one or more of our major resellers or end-user customers with revenues from others in future periods, our revenues may decline and our growth would be limited.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Historically, in any given year a single customer, either reseller or end-user customer, could account for more than 10% of our revenue.&#160;&#160;In fiscal year 2017, two resellers, SHI International Corp. and Synnex Information Technologies, accounted for approximately 20% and 16% of our total revenue, respectively.&#160; Synnex Information Technologies and Prosys Information Systems, Inc., accounted for approximately 23% and 11% of our total revenue, respectively, for fiscal year 2016.&#160;&#160;If we are unable to replace revenues generated from one or more of our major resellers or end-user customers with revenues from others, our revenues may decline and our growth could be limited.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; FONT-STYLE: italic; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">We experience lengthy sales cycles for our products and the delay of an expected large order could result in a significant unexpected revenue shortfall.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">The purchase of our rugged computer systems is often an enterprise-wide decision for prospective end-user customers, which requires us to engage in sales efforts over an extended period of time and provide a significant level of education to prospective end-user customers regarding the uses and benefits of such systems.&#160;&#160;As a result, our products generally have a lengthy sales cycle, ranging from several months to several years.&#160;&#160;Consequently, if forecasted sales from a specific end-user customer are not realized, we may not be able to generate revenue from alternative sources in time to compensate for the shortfall.&#160;&#160;The loss or delay of an expected large order could result in a significant unexpected revenue shortfall.&#160;&#160;Moreover, to the extent we enter into and perform significant contracts earlier than expected, operating results for subsequent periods may fall below expectations.</div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; FONT-STYLE: italic; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our quarterly operating results are likely to fluctuate as a result of many factors and our quarterly operating results may not be indicative of results in any given year or future quarter.</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">Our quarterly revenue, expenses, operating results, and gross profit margins may vary significantly from quarter to quarter.&#160; Such fluctuation may result from our inability to replace revenue generated from large orders from one of our major resellers or end-user customers with revenue from other resellers and end-users, the lengthy sales cycle related to our products and delays in the delivery of products and components.&#160; Our quarterly revenue could also be materially affected in any period by a decline in the economic prospects of our customers or the economy in general, which could alter current or prospective customers&#8217; spending priorities or budget cycles or extend our sales cycle for the period.&#160; For example, in fiscal year 2016, one of our largest end user customers was the subject of a labor strike, which was only recently settled.&#160; During the duration of that strike, our customer greatly reduced its orders for our products.&#160; Due to such factors, our quarterly operating results are likely to fluctuate and such results may not be indicative of our results in any given year or future quarter.&#160; As a result, our operating results may fall below the expectations of securities analysts and investors in some quarters, which could result in a decrease in the market price of our common stock.&#160; You should not rely on quarter-to-quarter comparisons of our results of operations as an indicator of our future results<font style="FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">.</font></div>

<div style="MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt"><br>
</div>

<div style="TEXT-ALIGN: left; BACKGROUND-COLOR: #ffffff; MARGIN-TOP: 3pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; FONT-FAMILY: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; MARGIN-BOTTOM: 6pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-SIZE: 10pt">The long-term success of the Motion Acquisition will depend in part on the success of our management in integrating the operations, technologies and personnel.&#160; Our management&#8217;s inability to meet the challenges involved in integrating successfully the operations or otherwise to realize the anticipated benefits of the Motion Acquisition could seriously harm our results of operations.&#160; In addition, the overall integration of the Motion operations into our company will continue to require substantial attention from our management, which could further harm our results of operations.<br>

<div style="TEXT-INDENT: 36pt">We may not be able to successfully integrate operations in a timely manner, or at all, and we may not realize the anticipated benefits of the Motion Acquisition, including potential cost and operating synergies or sales or growth opportunities, to the extent or in the time frame anticipated.&#160; The anticipated benefits and synergies of the Motion Acquisition are based on assumptions and current expectations, assume a successful integration and reallocation of resources among our facilities without unanticipated costs, and assume our efforts do not have unforeseen or unintended consequences.&#160; In addition, our ability to realize the benefits and synergies of the business combination could be adversely impacted to the extent that relationships with existing or potential customers, suppliers or strategic partners are adversely affected as a consequence of the acquisition or by practical or legal constraints on our ability to combine operations.<br>

<div><br>
